[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        if (on)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(q[k], M4_IRQ_PRIO, 0U);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(q[k]);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(q[k]);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            HAL_NVIC_DisableIRQ(q[k]);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            NVIC_ClearPendingIRQ(q[k]);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +/* ---- DMA1/DMA2 streams of the caller (02_dma) --------------------------------------------- */
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +static bool m4_stream(DMA_Stream_TypeDef *s, bool tx, uint8_t *mem, uint32_t n)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t req = 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_GetDmaRequest(I2C1, tx, &req);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    const DmaBm_ConfigEx_t d = {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        .base = { .stream = s, .request_id = req, .direction = tx ? DMA_BM_DIR_MEM_TO_PERIPH : DMA_BM_DIR_PERIPH_TO_MEM,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE, .mem_data_size = DMA_BM_DATA_SIZE_BYTE,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_inc = false, .mem_inc = true, .circular = false, .priority = DMA_BM_PRIORITY_HIGH,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .enable_it_ht = false, .enable_it_tc = false, .enable_it_te = false, .enable_it_dme = false,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .enable_it_fe = false, .enable_trbuff = false, .enable_fifo = false,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .fifo_threshold = DMA_BM_FIFO_TH_1_2,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .periph_addr = tx ? (uint32_t)&I2C1->TXDR : (uint32_t)&I2C1->RXDR, .mem_addr = (uint32_t)mem,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +                  .buffer_length = n },
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        .mem1_addr = 0U, .mem_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE, .periph_burst = DMA_BM_BURST_SINGLE,
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        .periph_flow_ctrl = false, .fail_if_busy = true, .stop_spin_limit = 0U };
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    return DMA_BM_InitStreamEx(&d) && DMA_BM_StartEx(s);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +static void m4_stream_end(DMA_Stream_TypeDef *s)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_BM_StopEx(s, 0U);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    (void)DMA_BM_DeinitStream(s);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t sentinel_left(uint32_t n)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t left = 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        if (s_buf[i] == M4_SENTINEL)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            left++;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    return left;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +/* One transfer on I2C1, initialised by M4_INIT. Writes: s_buf[0..n-1] from the
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +   seed (the first byte is the target's pointer); reads and write-reads: the
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +   target's known content from pointer `p` (IST_TARGET_REG). */
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_xfer(uint32_t xf, uint32_t n, uint32_t arg, uint32_t max_cycles)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t op = IST_XF_OP(xf);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mode = IST_XF_MODE(xf);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t addr = IST_XF_ADDR(xf);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    if ((n > M4_BUF_N) || (max_cycles == 0U))
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_STATUS] = (uint32_t)I2C_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    const bool rd = (op == IST_OP_READ) || (op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        ist_fill(s_buf, n, arg);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            s_buf[i] = M4_SENTINEL;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    s_ptr_byte = (uint8_t)arg;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t done_tx = 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t done_rx = 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t aux = 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    if (mode == IST_MODE_POLL)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Write(I2C1, addr, s_buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        else if (op == IST_OP_READ)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Read(I2C1, addr, s_buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        else if (op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD)
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_WriteRead(I2C1, addr, &s_ptr_byte, 1U, s_buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Write(I2C1, addr, NULL, 0U, max_cycles);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        done_tx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? n : ((op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD) ? 1U : 0U);
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        done_rx = rd ? n : 0U;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    else if ((mode == IST_MODE_IT) || (mode == IST_MODE_IT_CTRL))
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        s_it.addr7 = addr;
[+   1.806s] [host] diff| +        s_it.tx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? s_buf : &s_ptr_byte;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it.ntx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? n : ((op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD) ? 1U : 0U);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it.rx = s_buf;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it.nrx = rd ? n : 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        nvic_i2c1(true);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it_irqs = 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it_active = true;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        st = I2C_BM_StartIt(I2C1, &s_it);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        if (mode == IST_MODE_IT_CTRL)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            /* T6's control: every enable cleared by hand before the first event. */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            __disable_irq();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            I2C1->CR1 &= ~(I2C_CR1_TXIE | I2C_CR1_RXIE | I2C_CR1_TCIE | I2C_CR1_STOPIE | I2C_CR1_NACKIE | I2C_CR1_ERRIE);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        if (st != I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t last = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t same = 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        while (s_it_active && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < max_cycles))
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            same = (now == last) ? (same + 1U) : 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            last = now;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            if (same > STALL_READS)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                IST_AREA->m4_stalls++;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                break;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        const bool timed_out = s_it_active;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        s_it_active = false;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        if (st == I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            st = timed_out ? I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT : s_it.status;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_IRQS] = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        done_tx = s_it.done_tx;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        done_rx = s_it.done_rx;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t before = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        (void)delay_cyc(SystemCoreClock / 100U);          /* T6: 10 ms with no I2C1 IRQ */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_IRQS_AFTER] = s_it_irqs - before;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        nvic_i2c1(false);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        if (timed_out)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            aux = (uint32_t)s_it.status;                   /* still PENDING: no progress */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        /* DMA (plan rule 8): the stream first, then DmaStart, WaitEnd, the stream
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +           stopped, DmaEnd. */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        bool sok;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            sok = m4_stream(M4_DMA_TX, true, &s_buf[1], n - 1U);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            const I2cBm_Status_t s1 = I2C_BM_DmaStartWrite(I2C1, addr, s_buf, n);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            st = (s1 == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_WaitEnd(I2C1, n, max_cycles) : s1;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            m4_stream_end(M4_DMA_TX);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            done_tx = n;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            sok = m4_stream(M4_DMA_RX, false, s_buf, n);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            const I2cBm_Status_t s1 = I2C_BM_DmaStartRead(I2C1, addr, n);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            if ((s1 == I2C_BM_OK) && (mode == IST_MODE_DMA_CTRL))
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();      /* T7's control: RXDMAEN cleared by hand */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                __disable_irq();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                I2C1->CR1 &= ~I2C_CR1_RXDMAEN;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +                __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            st = (s1 == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_WaitEnd(I2C1, n, max_cycles) : s1;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t w0 = DWT->CYCCNT;               /* the last byte leaves RXDR */
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            while ((DMA_BM_GetRemaining(M4_DMA_RX) != 0U) && ((DWT->CYCCNT - w0) < (SystemCoreClock / 1000U)))
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            done_rx = n - DMA_BM_GetRemaining(M4_DMA_RX);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +            m4_stream_end(M4_DMA_RX);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        const I2cBm_Status_t se = I2C_BM_DmaEnd(I2C1);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        aux = (sok ? 0U : 1U) | ((se == I2C_BM_OK) ? 0U : 2U);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    if ((mode == IST_MODE_POLL))
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t first = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_STATUS] = (uint32_t)st;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_CRC] = rd ? ist_crc32(s_buf, n) : ((op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? ist_crc32(s_buf, n) : 0U);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_MISM] = rd ? ist_check_target(s_buf, n, arg & 0xFFU, &first) : 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_FIRST_BAD] = first;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_DONE_TX] = done_tx;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_DONE_RX] = done_rx;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_CR1_AFTER] = I2C1->CR1;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_SENTINEL] = rd ? sentinel_left(n) : 0U;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_ISR_AFTER] = I2C1->ISR;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_AUX] = aux;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_t1(void)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    static IstT1Result_t t;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C1);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    ist_t1(I2C1, IST_AREA->arg0, &t);
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[0] = t.cases;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = t.bad;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[2] = t.groups_same;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    r[3] = t.control_changed;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < t.cases) && (i < IST_T1_MAX); i++)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +        r[4U + (i / 4U)] |= ((uint32_t)t.got[i] & 0xFFU) << (8U * (i % 4U));
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_busy(void)
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    static IstT1Result_t t;
[+   1.807s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    ist_t1_busy(I2C1, &t);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[0] = t.cases;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = t.bad;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[2] = t.groups_same;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[3] = t.isr;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < t.cases) && (i < IST_T1_MAX); i++)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        r[4U + (i / 4U)] |= ((uint32_t)t.got[i] & 0xFFU) << (8U * (i % 4U));
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_t2(void)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    IstT2Result_t t;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C1);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    ist_t2(I2C1, &t);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[0] = t.cases;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = t.bad;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[2] = t.bad_deinit;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[3] = t.first_bad;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[4] = t.got;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[5] = t.exp;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[6] = t.control;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[7] = t.bad_status;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +/* T13: this core's bank only (RM0399 9.5.10), I2C2 enabled in CPU1's bank. */
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_t13(uint32_t what)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    if (what == 2U)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        r[0] = (uint32_t)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[1] = (uint32_t)I2C_BM_DisableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    r[2] = RCC_C2->APB1LENR;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_fmp(uint32_t on)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +/* T12 (ES0445 2.19.6): `count` writes of 2 bytes, I2C1 initialised on the HSI kernel
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +   clock by CPU1 (APB1 / kernel within 1.5..3). The driver preloads the first byte;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +   the manual variants write CR2 themselves without it, the control of the erratum. */
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +static I2cBm_Status_t t12_manual(bool dma, uint32_t max_cycles)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +{

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    I2C1->ISR = I2C_ISR_TXE;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    if (dma)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    while ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < max_cycles)

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        if (!dma && ((isr & I2C_ISR_TXIS) != 0U) && (sent < 2U))
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            sent++;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        if ((isr & I2C_ISR_STOPF) != 0U)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_OK;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            break;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    if (dma)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        m4_stream_end(M4_DMA_TX);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        I2C1->CR1 &= ~I2C_CR1_TXDMAEN;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    }

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        /* Stalled: only PE = 0 releases it (ES0445 2.19.6). */
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        I2C1->CR1 &= ~I2C_CR1_PE;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < 1000U) && ((I2C1->CR1 & I2C_CR1_PE) != 0U); i++)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        I2C1->CR1 |= I2C_CR1_PE;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    }

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    I2C1->CR2 = 0U;

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +static void cmd_t12(uint32_t what, uint32_t count, uint32_t max_cycles)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    volatile uint32_t *r = res();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    res_clear();
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ok = 0U;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t stalled = 0U;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t other = 0U;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t slow = 0U;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    s_buf[0] = 0xF0U;                                    /* the target's pointer: 0xF0 */

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        I2cBm_Status_t st;
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        if (what == IST_T12_POLL_DRIVER)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Write(I2C1, IST_TARGET_ADDR, s_buf, 2U, max_cycles);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        else if (what == IST_T12_DMA_DRIVER)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +        {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_DmaStartWrite(I2C1, IST_TARGET_ADDR, s_buf, 2U);
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            if (st == I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            {

[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.808s] [host] diff| +            m4_stream_end(M4_DMA_TX);
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +            (void)I2C_BM_DmaEnd(I2C1);

[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        else

[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        slow = (dt > slow) ? dt : slow;
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        if (st == I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +            ok++;
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +            stalled++;
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        else if (other == 0U)
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +            other = 0x100U | (uint32_t)st;
[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.809s] [host] diff| +    r[3] = slow;

[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +/* The CYCCNT budget of a transfer of `bytes` bytes at f Hz: 1.6 times its nine bits per
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +   byte plus 20 ms (the target's own clock stretching, the reloads), within 32 bits. */
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t budget(uint32_t cpu_hz, uint32_t f, uint32_t bytes)
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +    const uint64_t bus = ((uint64_t)(bytes + 2U) * 9U * (uint64_t)cpu_hz * 16U) / ((uint64_t)f * 10U);
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +    const uint64_t c = bus + ((uint64_t)cpu_hz / 50U);
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +    return (c > 0xF0000000ULL) ? 0xF0000000UL : (uint32_t)c;
[+   1.810s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +static void it_serve(void)
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +    s_it_irqs++;

[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        const bool done = I2C_BM_XferIrq(I2C4, &s_it);
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        s_irq_min = (dt < s_irq_min) ? dt : s_irq_min;
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        s_irq_max = (dt > s_irq_max) ? dt : s_irq_max;
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        if (done)
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +    else

[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.811s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +/* One transfer on I2C4 (initialised), the twin of CPU2's cmd_xfer(): polling and IT on
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +   s_big (AXI SRAM, CPU only), DMA on s_bd_tx/s_bd_rx (SRAM4, BDMA, non-cacheable). */
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +static void cm7_xfer(uint32_t xf, uint32_t n, uint32_t arg, uint32_t max_cycles, uint32_t r[IST_XR_N])
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IST_XR_N; k++)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        r[k] = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t op = IST_XF_OP(xf);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t mode = IST_XF_MODE(xf);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t addr = IST_XF_ADDR(xf);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    const bool dma = (mode == IST_MODE_DMA) || (mode == IST_MODE_DMA_CTRL);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t *buf = dma ? ((op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? s_bd_tx : s_bd_rx) : s_big;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    if ((n > (dma ? BD_N : F1_BIG)) || (max_cycles == 0U))
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_STATUS] = (uint32_t)I2C_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    const bool rd = (op == IST_OP_READ) || (op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        ist_fill(buf, n, arg);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            buf[i] = SENTINEL;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    s_ptrb = (uint8_t)arg;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_ERR_PARAM;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t done_tx = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t done_rx = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t aux = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    if (mode == IST_MODE_POLL)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Write(I2C4, addr, buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        else if (op == IST_OP_READ)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Read(I2C4, addr, buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        else if (op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_WriteRead(I2C4, addr, &s_ptrb, 1U, buf, n, max_cycles);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            st = I2C_BM_Write(I2C4, addr, NULL, 0U, max_cycles);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        done_tx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? n : ((op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD) ? 1U : 0U);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        done_rx = rd ? n : 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    else if ((mode == IST_MODE_IT) || (mode == IST_MODE_IT_CTRL))
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it.addr7 = addr;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it.tx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? buf : &s_ptrb;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it.ntx = (op == IST_OP_WRITE) ? n : ((op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD) ? 1U : 0U);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it.rx = buf;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it.nrx = rd ? n : 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        nvic_i2c4(true);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it_irqs = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it_active = true;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        st = I2C_BM_StartIt(I2C4, &s_it);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        if (mode == IST_MODE_IT_CTRL)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            /* T6's control: every enable cleared by hand before the first event. */
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            __disable_irq();
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            I2C4->CR1 &= ~(I2C_CR1_TXIE | I2C_CR1_RXIE | I2C_CR1_TCIE | I2C_CR1_STOPIE | I2C_CR1_NACKIE | I2C_CR1_ERRIE);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        if (st != I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            s_it_active = false;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t last = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t same = 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        while (s_it_active && ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < max_cycles))
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            same = (now == last) ? (same + 1U) : 0U;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            last = now;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            if (same > STALL_READS)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +                s_stalls++;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +                break;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        const bool timed_out = s_it_active;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        s_it_active = false;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        if (st == I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +            st = timed_out ? I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT : s_it.status;
[+   1.812s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_IRQS] = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        done_tx = s_it.done_tx;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        done_rx = s_it.done_rx;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t before = s_it_irqs;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        (void)delay_ms(10U);                                 /* T6: 10 ms with no I2C4 IRQ */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        r[IST_XR_IRQS_AFTER] = s_it_irqs - before;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        nvic_i2c4(false);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        if (timed_out)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            aux = (uint32_t)s_it.status;                     /* still PENDING: no progress */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        /* BDMA through dma_hal (plan rule 8): the channel first, then DmaStart, WaitEnd,
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +           the channel aborted, DmaEnd. */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        bool sok;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        if (op == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            sok = bd_init(&s_hbd_tx, BDMA_Channel1, true) &&
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                  (HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_tx, (uint32_t)&buf[1], (uint32_t)&I2C4->TXDR, n - 1U) == HAL_OK);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            const I2cBm_Status_t s1 = I2C_BM_DmaStartWrite(I2C4, addr, buf, n);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            st = (s1 == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_WaitEnd(I2C4, n, max_cycles) : s1;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_tx);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            done_tx = n;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        else
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            sok = bd_init(&s_hbd_rx, BDMA_Channel0, false) &&
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                  (HAL_DMA_Start(&s_hbd_rx, (uint32_t)&I2C4->RXDR, (uint32_t)buf, n) == HAL_OK);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            const I2cBm_Status_t s1 = I2C_BM_DmaStartRead(I2C4, addr, n);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            if ((s1 == I2C_BM_OK) && (mode == IST_MODE_DMA_CTRL))
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();         /* T7's control: RXDMAEN cleared by hand */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                __disable_irq();
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                I2C4->CR1 &= ~I2C_CR1_RXDMAEN;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            st = (s1 == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_WaitEnd(I2C4, n, max_cycles) : s1;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            r[IST_XR_CYCLES] = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            if (st == I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                (void)HAL_DMA_PollForTransfer(&s_hbd_rx, HAL_DMA_FULL_TRANSFER, 10U);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            done_rx = n - __HAL_DMA_GET_COUNTER(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            (void)HAL_DMA_Abort(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            (void)DMA_HAL_DeInit(&s_hbd_rx);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        const I2cBm_Status_t se = I2C_BM_DmaEnd(I2C4);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        aux = (sok ? 0U : 1U) | ((se == I2C_BM_OK) ? 0U : 2U);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t first = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_STATUS] = (uint32_t)st;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_CRC] = (rd || (op == IST_OP_WRITE)) ? ist_crc32(buf, n) : 0U;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_MISM] = rd ? ist_check_target(buf, n, arg & 0xFFU, &first) : 0U;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_FIRST_BAD] = first;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_DONE_TX] = done_tx;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_DONE_RX] = done_rx;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_CR1_AFTER] = I2C4->CR1;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_SENTINEL] = rd ? sentinel_left(buf, n) : 0U;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_ISR_AFTER] = I2C4->ISR;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    r[IST_XR_AUX] = aux;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +/* A transfer on CPU2's I2C1 (m4) or this core's I2C4; r receives IST_XR_*. The bytes of
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +   a write are regenerated here from the seed for the X line (both cores fill the same). */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +static bool xfer(bool m4, uint32_t xf, uint32_t n, uint32_t arg, uint32_t max_cycles, uint32_t r[IST_XR_N])
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    if (!m4)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        cm7_xfer(xf, n, arg, max_cycles, r);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        return true;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    if (!cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_XFER, xf, n, arg, max_cycles))
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IST_XR_N; k++)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        r[k] = IST_AREA->m4_res[k];
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +/* The X line of one transfer: what the host pairs with the target's RPT (t=1). For a
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +   write of up to 8 bytes, the bytes too (the host compares them with the scope). */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +static void xline(const char *test, bool m4, uint32_t f, uint32_t xf, uint32_t n, uint32_t arg, const uint32_t *r,
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +                  bool target)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    char d[24] = "";
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t op = IST_XF_OP(xf);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    if ((op == IST_OP_WRITE) && (n <= 8U))
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        uint8_t b[8];
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        ist_fill(b, n, arg);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +            (void)snprintf(&d[2U * i], 3U, "%02X", b[i]);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    log_long("X %s %s f=%lu op=%s mode=%s a=%02lX n=%lu p=%lu st=%lu crc=%08lX mis=%lu bad=%ld cyc=%lu dtx=%lu drx=%lu "
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             "irq=%lu irqa=%lu cr1=%08lX sen=%lu isr=%08lX aux=%lu t=%u%s%s", test, m4 ? "cm4" : "cm7", (unsigned long)f,
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             op_name(op), mode_name(IST_XF_MODE(xf)), (unsigned long)IST_XF_ADDR(xf), (unsigned long)n,
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)arg, (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_STATUS], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_CRC],
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_MISM], (long)(int32_t)r[IST_XR_FIRST_BAD], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_CYCLES],
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_DONE_TX], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_DONE_RX], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_IRQS],
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_IRQS_AFTER], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_CR1_AFTER], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_SENTINEL],
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_ISR_AFTER], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_AUX], target ? 1U : 0U,
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +             (d[0] != '\0') ? " d=" : "", d);
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +}

[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +/* Init of the instance under test on either core; the image read back. */
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +static I2cBm_Status_t inst_init(bool m4, uint32_t timingr, uint32_t tmo, uint32_t img[IST_NREG])
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        if (!cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_INIT, timingr, 1U, tmo, 0U))
[+   1.813s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            return I2C_BM_ERR_HAL;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IST_NREG; k++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            img[k] = IST_AREA->m4_res[1U + k];
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        return (I2cBm_Status_t)IST_AREA->m4_res[0];
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Config_t c = { I2C4, timingr, true, 0U, tmo };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C4);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    ist_regs(I2C4, img);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    return st;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +/* Deinit, then T14: the reset image of RM0399 50.7 typed in Common (ist_reset_diff()). */
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +static bool inst_deinit(bool m4, uint32_t *diff)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_Status_t st;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        if (!cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_DEINIT, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            *diff = 0xFFU;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            return false;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        st = (I2cBm_Status_t)IST_AREA->m4_res[0];
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IST_NREG; k++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            img[k] = IST_AREA->m4_res[1U + k];
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        st = I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C4);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        ist_regs(I2C4, img);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t rst[IST_NREG] = { 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0x00000001UL };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t d = 0U;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < IST_NREG; k++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        if (img[k] != rst[k])
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            d |= 1UL << k;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    *diff = d;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    return (st == I2C_BM_OK) && (d == 0U);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T3: CalcTiming, printed for the host to recompute (C15) -------------------------- */
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t s_b1_calc_max;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +static void t3_one(uint32_t *i, uint32_t k, uint32_t f, uint32_t tr, uint32_t tf, bool af, uint32_t dnf)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_TimingIn_t in = { k, f, tr, tf, af, dnf };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_CalcTiming(&in, &o);
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t dt = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    s_b1_calc_max = (dt > s_b1_calc_max) ? dt : s_b1_calc_max;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    LOG("T3 %lu %lu %lu %lu %lu %u %lu %u %08lX %lu %lu", (unsigned long)*i, (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)f,
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)tr, (unsigned long)tf, (unsigned)af, (unsigned long)dnf, (unsigned)st,
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)((st == I2C_BM_OK) ? o.timingr : 0U), (unsigned long)((st == I2C_BM_OK) ? o.mode : 0U),
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)((st == I2C_BM_OK) ? o.scl_hz_model : 0U));
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    (*i)++;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t3(void)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ks[] = { 4000000U, 8000000U, 10000000U, 16000000U, 20000000U, 48000000U, 64000000U,
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +                                   100000000U, 125000000U };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t fs[] = { 10000U, 100000U, 250000U, 400000U, 1000000U };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t rf[][2] = { { 100U, 10U }, { 250U, 20U }, { 300U, 300U }, { 120U, 120U } };
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t n = 0U;
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < (sizeof(ks) / sizeof(ks[0])); a++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t b = 0U; b < (sizeof(fs) / sizeof(fs[0])); b++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t c = 0U; c < (sizeof(rf) / sizeof(rf[0])); c++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t d = 0U; d < 2U; d++)
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +                    t3_one(&n, ks[a], fs[b], rf[c][0], rf[c][1], d != 0U, 0U);

[+   1.814s] [host] diff| +{
