[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +/* Silicon facts of the table against the CMSIS/HAL constants, and the kernel field
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +   against RCC read directly (C1, plan rule 2). */
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +static void f1_caps(void)
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    I2C_TypeDef *const inst[4] = { I2C1, I2C2, I2C3, I2C4 };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    static const IRQn_Type ev[4] = { I2C1_EV_IRQn, I2C2_EV_IRQn, I2C3_EV_IRQn, I2C4_EV_IRQn };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    static const IRQn_Type er[4] = { I2C1_ER_IRQn, I2C2_ER_IRQn, I2C3_ER_IRQn, I2C4_ER_IRQn };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t rx[4] = { DMA_REQUEST_I2C1_RX, DMA_REQUEST_I2C2_RX, DMA_REQUEST_I2C3_RX, BDMA_REQUEST_I2C4_RX };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t tx[4] = { DMA_REQUEST_I2C1_TX, DMA_REQUEST_I2C2_TX, DMA_REQUEST_I2C3_TX, BDMA_REQUEST_I2C4_TX };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d2 = RCC->D2CCIP2R;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d3 = RCC->D3CCIPR;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    /* Field positions typed from RM0399 9.7.20 (I2C123SEL 13:12) and 9.7.21 (I2C4SEL 9:8). */
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t raw[4] = { (d2 >> 12) & 3U, (d2 >> 12) & 3U, (d2 >> 12) & 3U, (d3 >> 8) & 3U };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        I2cBm_Caps_t c = { 0xFFU, 0xFFU };
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        IRQn_Type e = (IRQn_Type)0;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        IRQn_Type f = (IRQn_Type)0;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t qr = 0U;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t qt = 0U;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t ks = 9U;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        const bool ok = I2C_BM_IsValidInstance(inst[i]) && (I2C_BM_GetCaps(inst[i], &c) == I2C_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                        (I2C_BM_GetIRQn(inst[i], &e, &f) == I2C_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                        (I2C_BM_GetDmaRequest(inst[i], false, &qr) == I2C_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                        (I2C_BM_GetDmaRequest(inst[i], true, &qt) == I2C_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                        (I2C_BM_GetKernelSource(inst[i], &ks) == I2C_BM_OK);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        const bool facts = (e == ev[i]) && (f == er[i]) && (qr == rx[i]) && (qt == tx[i]) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                           (c.dmamux == ((i == 3U) ? 2U : 1U)) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                           (c.kernel_select == ((i == 3U) ? I2C_BM_KSEL_I2C4 : I2C_BM_KSEL_I2C123)) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                           (ks == raw[i]) && (ks == 0U);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        if (!ok || !facts)
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            bad |= 1UL << i;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const bool not_inst = !I2C_BM_IsValidInstance((const I2C_TypeDef *)((uint32_t)I2C1 + 4U)) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +                          !I2C_BM_IsValidInstance((const I2C_TypeDef *)USART3) && !I2C_BM_IsValidInstance(NULL);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && not_inst,
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            "T1/T2 capability table of I2C1..4 against stm32h755xx.h (IRQn EV/ER), stm32h7xx_hal_dma.h (DMAMUX1 "
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            "33/34 35/36 73/74, DMAMUX2 13/14) and RCC read directly (I2C123SEL %lu, I2C4SEL %lu, 0 in prj_01): "
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            "rows that differ 0x%lX; I2C1+4, USART3 and NULL not instances %u", (unsigned long)raw[0],
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)raw[3], (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned)not_inst);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +/* SetFastModePlus: one bit of SYSCFG_PMCR, the rest unchanged, restored (C10, rule 10). */
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +static void f1_fmp(void)
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t before = SYSCFG->PMCR;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t s1 = I2C_BM_SetFastModePlus(I2C2, true);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t on = SYSCFG->PMCR;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t s2 = I2C_BM_SetFastModePlus(I2C2, false);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t off = SYSCFG->PMCR;
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +    verdict((s1 == I2C_BM_OK) && (s2 == I2C_BM_OK) && (on == (before | SYSCFG_PMCR_I2C2_FMP)) &&
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            (off == (before & ~SYSCFG_PMCR_I2C2_FMP)) && (off == before),
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            "SetFastModePlus(I2C2): SYSCFG_PMCR 0x%08lX -> on 0x%08lX (bit 1 only) -> off 0x%08lX (as before), "
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +            "statuses %u %u", (unsigned long)before, (unsigned long)on, (unsigned long)off, (unsigned)s1, (unsigned)s2);
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.815s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +/* T13: CPU2's DisableClock(I2C2) while CPU1's bank enables it (RM0399 9.5.10). The
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +   oracle is whether a register write lands: an unclocked I2C ignores it. */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t13(void)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)bench_timing(s_k123, 100000U, &o);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Config_t c = { I2C2, o.timingr, true, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2C2->TIMINGR = o.timingr;                             /* PE = 0: written directly */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t c1on = RCC_C1->APB1LENR & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    bool m1 = false;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    bool m2 = false;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r1 = 0U;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r2 = 0U;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_T13, 1U, 0U, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +        m1 = IST_AREA->m4_res[1] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +        r1 = IST_AREA->m4_res[2];
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2C2->TIMEOUTR = 0x00000123UL;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const bool land1 = (I2C2->TIMEOUTR == 0x00000123UL) && (I2C2->TIMINGR == o.timingr);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    if (cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_T13, 2U, 0U, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +        m2 = (IST_AREA->m4_res[0] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK) && (IST_AREA->m4_res[1] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +        r2 = IST_AREA->m4_res[2];
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2C2->TIMEOUTR = 0x00000456UL;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const bool land2 = (I2C2->TIMEOUTR == 0x00000456UL);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: CPU1 disables its bank too; nobody enables I2C2; a write is lost. */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t dc = I2C_BM_DisableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t both = (RCC_C1->APB1LENR | RCC_C2->APB1LENR) & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2C2->TIMINGR = 0x10101010UL;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t after = I2C2->TIMINGR;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_DisableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    verdict(m1 && land1 && ((r1 & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN) == 0U) && (c1on != 0U),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "T13 CPU2 DisableClock(I2C2) (RCC_C2 APB1LENR I2C2EN %lu) while RCC_C1 enables it (%lu): a TIMEOUTR write "
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "still lands %u", (unsigned long)((r1 & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN) != 0U), (unsigned long)(c1on != 0U),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)land1);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    verdict(m2 && land2 && ((r2 & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN) == 0U),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "T13 CPU2 EnableClock + DisableClock(I2C2): RCC_C2 I2C2EN %lu after; a write still lands %u",
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)((r2 & RCC_APB1LENR_I2C2EN) != 0U), (unsigned)land2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    verdict((dc == I2C_BM_OK) && (both == 0U) && (after == o.timingr),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "T13 control: CPU1 DisableClock(I2C2) %u, both banks off; TIMINGR 0x10101010 written then, read with the "
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "clock back: 0x%08lX (the earlier 0x%08lX: the write was lost)", (unsigned)dc, (unsigned long)after,
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)o.timingr);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +/* T15 (lesson E2): I2C2 has no pin in AF on this bench. A Write must end, bounded,
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +   and Deinit give the reset image. Control: I2C4 with its pins (step F1_CM7). */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t15(void)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)bench_timing(s_k123, 100000U, &o);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Config_t c = { I2C2, o.timingr, true, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t si = I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t isr0 = I2C2->ISR;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t b[2] = { 0x11U, 0x22U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t max = ms_cyc(5U);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sw = (si == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_Write(I2C2, T_ADDR, b, 2U, max) : si;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cw = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    ist_regs(I2C2, img);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sr = (si == I2C_BM_OK) ? I2C_BM_Read(I2C2, T_ADDR, b, 2U, max) : si;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cr = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sd = I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t diff = ist_reset_diff(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_DisableClock(I2C2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    log_long("T15 I2C2 without pins: Init %u, ISR after Init 0x%08lX; Write of 2 bytes %u in %lu cycles; registers "
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +             "after: CR1 0x%08lX CR2 0x%08lX ISR 0x%08lX; Read of 2 bytes %u in %lu cycles", (unsigned)si,
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)isr0, (unsigned)sw, (unsigned long)cw, (unsigned long)img[0], (unsigned long)img[1],
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)img[6], (unsigned)sr, (unsigned long)cr);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const bool bounded = (cw <= (max + ms_cyc(1U))) && (cr <= (max + ms_cyc(1U)));
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    verdict(((sw == I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT) || (sw == I2C_BM_ERR_BUS)) && ((sr == I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT) || (sr == I2C_BM_ERR_BUS)) &&
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            bounded && (sd == I2C_BM_OK) && (diff == 0U),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "T15 I2C2 without pins: Write %u, Read %u (ERR_TIMEOUT %u or ERR_BUS %u), each within max_cycles + 1 ms "
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "(%lu, %lu <= %lu); Deinit %u, registers that differ from reset 0x%02lX", (unsigned)sw, (unsigned)sr,
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT, (unsigned)I2C_BM_ERR_BUS, (unsigned long)cw, (unsigned long)cr,
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(max + ms_cyc(1U)), (unsigned)sd, (unsigned long)diff);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +/* T14's control: an instance left initialised is told apart from the reset image. */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +static void f1_t14_control(void)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)bench_timing(s_k123, 100000U, &o);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Config_t c = { I2C3, o.timingr, true, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C3);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d1 = ist_reset_diff(I2C3);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sd = I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C3);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d2 = ist_reset_diff(I2C3);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_DisableClock(I2C3);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    verdict((d1 != 0U) && (sd == I2C_BM_OK) && (d2 == 0U),
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "T14 control: I2C3 initialised and not deinitialised differs from the reset image in 0x%02lX (CR1, "
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +            "TIMINGR); after Deinit %u: 0x%02lX", (unsigned long)d1, (unsigned)sd, (unsigned long)d2);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +/* The CPU clock against the AD2's (plan N1): MCU_ACK high for exactly CLKREF_CYC CYCCNT
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +   cycles, interrupts masked; the host times it with the logic analyzer. */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +#define CLKREF_CYC   40000000UL       /* 100 ms at 400 MHz nominal */
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +static void f1_clkref(void)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("CLKREF cyc=%lu cpu=%lu", (unsigned long)CLKREF_CYC, (unsigned long)s_cpu_hz))
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    (void)delay_ms(50U);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    ack(true);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    while ((DWT->CYCCNT - t0) < CLKREF_CYC)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    ack(false);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    LOG("CLKREF done");
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +static void step_f1_nopin(void)
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("F1_NOPIN"))
[+   1.816s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_clocks();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_clkref();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t3();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_caps();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t1();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t2();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_fmp();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t13();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t15();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    f1_t14_control();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F1_NOPIN end"))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    step_end();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +/* ---- Transfers on the bus (steps F1_CM4 and F1_CM7) ------------------------------------- */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t core_hz(bool m4)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    return m4 ? (s_cpu_hz / 2U) : s_cpu_hz;              /* CPU2 at 200 MHz (CM7 main.c) */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +static bool pace(void)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    return delay_ms(PACE_MS);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +/* One transfer: done, printed, paced. ok_st is the status it must end with. */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +static bool run1(const char *test, bool m4, uint32_t f, uint32_t xf, uint32_t n, uint32_t arg, bool target,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +                 I2cBm_Status_t ok_st, uint32_t *bad, uint32_t r[IST_XR_N])
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t op = IST_XF_OP(xf);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t bytes = n + ((op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD) ? 2U : 1U);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!xfer(m4, xf, n, arg, budget(core_hz(m4), f, bytes), r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("CM4 transfer command");
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    xline(test, m4, f, xf, n, arg, r, target);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    const bool rd = (op == IST_OP_READ) || (op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if ((r[IST_XR_STATUS] != (uint32_t)ok_st) || (rd && (ok_st == I2C_BM_OK) && (r[IST_XR_MISM] != 0U)))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        (*bad)++;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    return pace();
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +/* T4 at one speed on one core (CPU2's I2C1 or CPU1's I2C4): reads from the target's
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +   known content, write-reads, the address probes, writes; the control (a returned byte
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +   inverted by the target) last. The host pairs every X line with t=1 with an RPT. */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +static void t4_speed(bool m4, uint32_t f, uint32_t timingr)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    const char *core = m4 ? "CM4" : "CM7";
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t p = 0U;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < T4_SIZES; i++)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t n = k_t4_sizes[i];
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        if (!run1("T4", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), n, p, true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        p = (p + n) & 0xFFU;                              /* the target's pointer moves by n */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < T4_SIZES; i++)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t ptr = (0x10U * (i + 1U)) & 0xFFU;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        if (!run1("T4", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITEREAD, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), k_t4_sizes[i], ptr, true, I2C_BM_OK,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +                  &bad, r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!run1("T4", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_PROBE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 0U, 0U, true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r) ||
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        !run1("T4", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_PROBE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_NOADDR), 0U, 0U, false, I2C_BM_ERR_NACK, &bad, r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < T4_SIZES; i++)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        if (!run1("T4", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), k_t4_sizes[i], next_seed(), true,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +                  I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T4 %s at %lu Hz (TIMINGR 0x%08lX): %lu reads, %lu write-reads, 2 probes, %lu writes of 1..65535 "
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            "bytes: %lu with another status or a byte that differs from the target's content", core, (unsigned long)f,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)timingr, (unsigned long)T4_SIZES, (unsigned long)T4_SIZES, (unsigned long)T4_SIZES,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: the target inverts the 6th byte it returns; the check sees it. */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT") || !host_at("F334 CORRUPT 5"))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t cbad = 0U;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!run1("T4C", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 16U, 0U, true, I2C_BM_OK, &cbad, r))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    verdict((r[IST_XR_STATUS] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK) && (r[IST_XR_MISM] == 1U) && (r[IST_XR_FIRST_BAD] == 5U),
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            "T4 %s control at %lu Hz: target armed CORRUPT 5, read of 16: %lu byte differs, at index %ld (5)", core,
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_MISM], (long)(int32_t)r[IST_XR_FIRST_BAD]);
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +/* T5 (taps): short transfers, each one captured by the scope; the host decodes it
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +   and judges the bytes, the conditions and the times against TIMINGR. */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +static void t5_speed(bool m4, uint32_t f, uint32_t timingr, uint32_t k, uint32_t model)
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    /* The reads first, while the target holds its known content and pointer 0; the
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +       writes (random bytes from a seed, the first one the pointer) after. */
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +    struct { uint32_t op; uint32_t addr; uint32_t n; uint32_t arg; bool target; I2cBm_Status_t st; bool probe_first; } c[5] = {
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        { IST_OP_READ, T_ADDR, 2U, 0U, true, I2C_BM_OK, false },
[+   1.817s] [host] diff| +        { IST_OP_WRITEREAD, T_ADDR, 1U, 0x07U, true, I2C_BM_OK, false },
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        { IST_OP_WRITE, T_ADDR, 1U, 0x00000020UL, true, I2C_BM_OK, true },     /* probe + 1 byte: tBUF  */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        { IST_OP_WRITE, T_ADDR, 2U, 0x0000C310UL, true, I2C_BM_OK, false },
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        { IST_OP_PROBE, T_NOADDR, 0U, 0U, false, I2C_BM_ERR_NACK, false },    /* NACK and STOP (T8(a)) */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    };
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 5U; i++)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (!host_at("SCOPE T5 k=%lu f=%lu kernel=%lu timingr=%08lX model=%lu rate=%lu trig=sda", (unsigned long)i,
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +                     (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)timingr, (unsigned long)model,
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +                     (unsigned long)((f <= 100000U) ? 20000000U : 50000000U)))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (c[i].probe_first)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            if (!xfer(m4, IST_XF(IST_OP_PROBE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 0U, 0U, budget(core_hz(m4), f, 1U), r))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            xline("T5", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_PROBE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 0U, 0U, r, true);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            bad += (r[IST_XR_STATUS] != (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (!xfer(m4, IST_XF(c[i].op, IST_MODE_POLL, c[i].addr), c[i].n, c[i].arg, budget(core_hz(m4), f, 4U), r))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        xline("T5", m4, f, IST_XF(c[i].op, IST_MODE_POLL, c[i].addr), c[i].n, c[i].arg, r, c[i].target);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        bad += ((r[IST_XR_STATUS] != (uint32_t)c[i].st) ||
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +                (((c[i].op == IST_OP_READ) || (c[i].op == IST_OP_WRITEREAD)) && (r[IST_XR_MISM] != 0U))) ? 1U : 0U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (!pace())
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("SCOPE end"))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    verdict(bad == 0U, "T5 %s at %lu Hz: 5 captured transfers (probe + write 1, write 2, read 2, write-read 1+1, probe "
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            "0x43) ended as expected on the H755 side: %lu did not (the host judges the bus)", m4 ? "CM4" : "CM7",
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)bad);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +/* The bench speeds of this session, one entry each: TIMINGR and its model. */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +static uint32_t speeds(uint32_t f_out[TAPS_SPEEDS])
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    if (s_taps)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        f_out[0] = 100000U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        f_out[1] = 400000U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        return 2U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    f_out[0] = 400000U;                                   /* also without taps: O1 (taps/notaps) */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    f_out[1] = 1000000U;                                  /* S19, D3: only without taps */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    return 2U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +/* Fm+ (SYSCFG_PMCR.I2CxFMP) of the instance under test, and the target's FMP. */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +static bool fmp(bool m4, bool on)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_Status_t st;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (!cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_FMP, on ? 1U : 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return false;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        st = (I2cBm_Status_t)IST_AREA->m4_res[0];
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        st = I2C_BM_SetFastModePlus(I2C4, on);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    LOG("Fm+ %s %s: SetFastModePlus %u, SYSCFG_PMCR 0x%08lX", m4 ? "I2C1" : "I2C4", on ? "on" : "off", (unsigned)st,
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)SYSCFG->PMCR);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    return (st == I2C_BM_OK) && host_at("F334 FMP %u", on ? 1U : 0U);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +/* T4 and T5 at every speed of the session. */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +static void t4_t5(bool m4)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t fl[TAPS_SPEEDS];
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t nf = speeds(fl);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t k = m4 ? s_k123 : s_k4;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < nf) && !s_bench_abort; i++)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        const I2cBm_Status_t sc = bench_timing(k, fl[i], &o);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        const bool fm = fl[i] > 400000U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (fm && !fmp(m4, true))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            bench_abort("Fm+");
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        const I2cBm_Status_t si = (sc == I2C_BM_OK) ? inst_init(m4, o.timingr, 0U, img) : sc;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        LOG("TIMING %s f=%lu kernel=%lu timingr=%08lX model=%lu calc=%u init=%u", m4 ? "CM4" : "CM7",
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)fl[i], (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)o.timingr, (unsigned long)o.scl_hz_model,
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)sc, (unsigned)si);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (si != I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            verdict(false, "Init of %s at %lu Hz: %u", m4 ? "I2C1" : "I2C4", (unsigned long)fl[i], (unsigned)si);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            bench_abort("Init");
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        t4_speed(m4, fl[i], o.timingr);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (s_taps)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            t5_speed(m4, fl[i], o.timingr, k, o.scl_hz_model);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t diff = 0U;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        const bool d_ok = inst_deinit(m4, &diff);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        verdict(d_ok, "T14 %s after T4/T5 at %lu Hz: Deinit, registers that differ from the reset image 0x%02lX",
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +                m4 ? "I2C1" : "I2C4", (unsigned long)fl[i], (unsigned long)diff);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        if (fm && !fmp(m4, false))
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            bench_abort("Fm+ off");
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +/* The main speed's TIMINGR for this core's instance, and Init. */
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +static bool main_init(bool m4, uint32_t tmo, uint32_t *timingr)
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sc = bench_timing(m4 ? s_k123 : s_k4, s_f_main, &o);
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.818s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t si = (sc == I2C_BM_OK) ? inst_init(m4, o.timingr, tmo, img) : sc;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    *timingr = o.timingr;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    if (si != I2C_BM_OK)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        verdict(false, "Init of %s at %lu Hz, timeout %lu: %u", m4 ? "I2C1" : "I2C4", (unsigned long)s_f_main,
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)tmo, (unsigned)si);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("Init");
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +/* T6: by interrupt; the control clears the enables by hand. */
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +static void t6(bool m4)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ns[4] = { 1U, 2U, 256U, 1000U };
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ops[3] = { IST_OP_WRITE, IST_OP_READ, IST_OP_WRITEREAD };
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t irq_left = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ie_left = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t f = s_f_main;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t p = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t o = 0U; o < 3U; o++)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            const uint32_t arg = (ops[o] == IST_OP_WRITE) ? next_seed() : ((ops[o] == IST_OP_READ) ? p : 0x40U);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            if (!run1("T6", m4, f, IST_XF(ops[o], IST_MODE_IT, T_ADDR), ns[i], arg, true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                return;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            irq_left += r[IST_XR_IRQS_AFTER];
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            ie_left |= r[IST_XR_CR1_AFTER] & 0xFEU;     /* every interrupt enable of CR1 */
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            if (ops[o] == IST_OP_READ)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                p = (p + ns[i]) & 0xFFU;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            if (ops[o] == IST_OP_WRITE)
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                /* writes change the target's registers: back to the known content */
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                    return;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +                p = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (irq_left == 0U) && (ie_left == 0U),
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            "T6 %s by interrupt at %lu Hz: write, read, write-read of 1, 2, 256, 1000 bytes: %lu with another status or "
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            "data; interrupt enables left in CR1 0x%02lX (0); IRQs in the 10 ms after the end %lu (0)",
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            m4 ? "CM4" : "CM7", (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned long)ie_left, (unsigned long)irq_left);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: the enables cleared before the first event: no progress, the wait
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +       ends by its budget; the instance is reset (the master held SCL low), and the
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +       target cuts its transfer after 500 ms without progress. */
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t max = ms_cyc(5U) / (m4 ? 2U : 1U);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    if (!xfer(m4, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_IT_CTRL, T_ADDR), 2U, next_seed(), max, r))
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    xline("T6C", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_IT_CTRL, T_ADDR), 2U, 0U, r, true);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t tr = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t diff = 0U;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    (void)inst_deinit(m4, &diff);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    (void)delay_ms(600U);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    const bool re = main_init(m4, 0U, &tr);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    (void)img;
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +    verdict((r[IST_XR_STATUS] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT) && (r[IST_XR_AUX] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_PENDING) &&
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            (r[IST_XR_IRQS] == 0U) && re,
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            "T6 %s control: enables cleared by hand after StartIt: status still PENDING (%lu), the wait ended by its "
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            "budget (%lu = ERR_TIMEOUT), %lu IRQs; Deinit, 600 ms (the target's cut), Init again %u", m4 ? "CM4" : "CM7",
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_AUX], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_STATUS], (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_IRQS], (unsigned)re);
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.819s] [host] diff| +/* T7: by DMA (CPU2: DMA2 streams through dma_bm; CPU1: BDMA through dma_hal, SRAM4). */
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +static void t7(bool m4)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    static const uint32_t ns[4] = { 2U, 255U, 256U, 4096U };
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t aux = 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t f = s_f_main;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t p = 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        if (!run1("T7", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_DMA, T_ADDR), ns[i], p, true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        aux |= r[IST_XR_AUX];
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        p = (p + ns[i]) & 0xFFU;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < 4U; i++)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        if (!run1("T7", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_DMA, T_ADDR), ns[i], next_seed(), true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        aux |= r[IST_XR_AUX];
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    verdict((bad == 0U) && (aux == 0U), "T7%s %s by %s at %lu Hz: read and write of 2, 255, 256, 4096 bytes: %lu with "
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            "another status or data; DMA steps not OK 0x%lX", m4 ? "(a)" : "(b)", m4 ? "CM4 I2C1" : "CM7 I2C4",
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            m4 ? "DMA2 (dma_bm)" : "BDMA (dma_hal, .d3_ram)", (unsigned long)f, (unsigned long)bad, (unsigned long)aux);
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: RXDMAEN cleared by hand: nobody reads RXDR, the buffer keeps its
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +       sentinel and WaitEnd ends by its budget (software reset). */
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t max = ms_cyc(5U) / (m4 ? 2U : 1U);
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!xfer(m4, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_DMA_CTRL, T_ADDR), 64U, 0U, max, r))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    xline("T7C", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_DMA_CTRL, T_ADDR), 64U, 0U, r, true);
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    (void)delay_ms(600U);                                 /* the target's 500 ms cut */
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    verdict((r[IST_XR_STATUS] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT) && (r[IST_XR_SENTINEL] == 64U),
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            "T7%s control: RXDMAEN cleared by hand, read of 64: WaitEnd %lu (ERR_TIMEOUT %u), bytes still holding the "
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            "sentinel %lu (64)", m4 ? "(a)" : "(b)", (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_STATUS], (unsigned)I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT,
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_SENTINEL]);
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +static bool wait_pin(uint32_t pin, uint32_t level, uint32_t ms)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t last = t0;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t same = 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    while (((GPIOB->IDR >> pin) & 1UL) != level)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t now = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        same = (now == last) ? (same + 1U) : 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        last = now;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        if ((same > STALL_READS) || ((now - t0) >= ms_cyc(ms)))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +            return false;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    return true;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +/* T8: (a) no target at 0x43; (b) the target NACKs data byte 3 of a write of 8. */
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +static void t8(bool m4)
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t bad = 0U;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t f = s_f_main;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!run1("T8A", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_NOADDR), 1U, 0x0000005AUL, false, I2C_BM_ERR_NACK,
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +              &bad, r))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t busy_a = r[IST_XR_ISR_AFTER] & I2C_ISR_BUSY;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!run1("T8A", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_PROBE, IST_MODE_IT, T_NOADDR), 0U, 0U, false, I2C_BM_ERR_NACK, &bad, r))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t busy_b = r[IST_XR_ISR_AFTER] & I2C_ISR_BUSY;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("F334 INIT"))
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.820s] [host] diff| +    if (!run1("T8A", m4, f, IST_XF(IST_OP_READ, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 4U, 0U, true, I2C_BM_OK, &bad, r))

[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +/* T1 with BUSY = 1: the target holds SDA low (a START on the bus); every transfer is

[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("HOLD SDA 100"))

[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    else

[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +/* B1: cycles of Init, of a Write of 1 byte, of XferIrq (min/max). */
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +static void b1(bool m4, uint32_t timingr)
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t ci;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t cw;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t qmin;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t qmax;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t sw;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    if (m4)
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        if (!cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_B1, timingr, 0U, 0U, 0U))
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +            return;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        ci = IST_AREA->m4_res[0];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        cw = IST_AREA->m4_res[1];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        qmin = IST_AREA->m4_res[2];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        qmax = IST_AREA->m4_res[3];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        sw = IST_AREA->m4_res[4];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    else
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        const I2cBm_Config_t c = { I2C4, timingr, true, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        uint8_t one = 0x80U;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        (void)I2C_BM_Deinit(I2C4);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        (void)I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        ci = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        sw = (uint32_t)I2C_BM_Write(I2C4, T_ADDR, &one, 1U, ms_cyc(10U));
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        cw = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        s_irq_min = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        s_irq_max = 0U;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        cm7_xfer(IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_IT, T_ADDR), 16U, 0x1234567UL, ms_cyc(10U), r);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        qmin = s_irq_min;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        qmax = s_irq_max;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    LOG("B1 %s at %lu Hz: I2C_BM_Init %lu cycles, I2C_BM_Write of 1 byte %lu cycles (status %lu), I2C_BM_XferIrq "
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        "%lu..%lu cycles per call (IT write of 16)", m4 ? "CM4 (200 MHz)" : "CM7 (400 MHz)", (unsigned long)s_f_main,
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +        (unsigned long)ci, (unsigned long)cw, (unsigned long)sw, (unsigned long)qmin, (unsigned long)qmax);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    (void)pace();
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T12 (ES0445 2.19.6) on CPU2's I2C1, the kernel clock on the HSI ------------------- */
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +#define T12_COUNT   1000U
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +static bool rcc_cr_set(uint32_t bit, bool on)
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    RCC->CR = on ? (RCC->CR | bit) : (RCC->CR & ~bit);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t rb = RCC->CR;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +    return ((rb & bit) != 0U) == on;
[+   1.822s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool hsi_ready(void)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    return (RCC->CR & RCC_CR_HSIRDY) != 0U;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +/* I2C123SEL [13:12] of RCC_D2CCIP2R (09:4744-4749): 00 pclk1, 10 hsi_ker_ck. Only
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +   in T12, read back (plan C10, instructions 3.5). */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool i2c123sel_set(uint32_t field)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t pm = __get_PRIMASK();
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    __disable_irq();
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    RCC->D2CCIP2R = (RCC->D2CCIP2R & ~RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Msk) | (field << RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Pos);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t rb = RCC->D2CCIP2R;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    __set_PRIMASK(pm);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    return ((rb & RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Msk) >> RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Pos) == field;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static void t12(void)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    static const char *const k_name[4] = { "poll, driver (first byte preloaded)", "DMA, driver (preloaded)",
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                                           "poll, by hand without preload", "DMA, by hand without preload" };
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cr_was = RCC->CR & RCC_CR_HSION;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t d2_was = RCC->D2CCIP2R;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t hsidiv = (RCC->CR & RCC_CR_HSIDIV_Msk) >> RCC_CR_HSIDIV_Pos;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t diff = 0U;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    (void)inst_deinit(true, &diff);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = rcc_cr_set(RCC_CR_HSION, true) && (wait_until(hsi_ready, 5U) == WAIT_DONE) && (hsidiv == 0U);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok && i2c123sel_set(2U);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t k = 64000000U;                        /* hsi_ker_ck, HSIDIV = /1 (09:3581-3586) */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    I2cBm_TimingOut_t o = { 0U, 0U, 0U };
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t img[IST_NREG];
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok && (bench_timing(k, s_f_main, &o) == I2C_BM_OK) && (inst_init(true, o.timingr, 0U, img) == I2C_BM_OK);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    log_long("T12 kernel: RCC_CR HSION was %lu, HSIDIV %lu (0: 64 MHz), HSIRDY %lu; RCC_D2CCIP2R 0x%08lX -> 0x%08lX "
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +             "(I2C123SEL = 10, HSI); APB1 %lu Hz / kernel %lu Hz = %lu.%02lu (ES0445 2.19.6 window 1.5..3); I2C1 "
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +             "TIMINGR 0x%08lX at %lu Hz", (unsigned long)(cr_was != 0U), (unsigned long)hsidiv,
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)hsi_ready(), (unsigned long)d2_was, (unsigned long)RCC->D2CCIP2R, (unsigned long)s_k123,
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)k, (unsigned long)(s_k123 / k), (unsigned long)(((s_k123 % k) * 100U) / k),
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +             (unsigned long)o.timingr, (unsigned long)s_f_main);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t res[4][4];
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    bool sent = ok;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t v = 0U; (v < 4U) && sent; v++)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        sent = host_at("F334 STATUS t12=%lu", (unsigned long)v) &&
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +               cm4_send4(IST_CMD_M4_T12, v, T12_COUNT, budget(s_cpu_hz / 2U, s_f_main, 3U) + ms_cyc(1U) / 2U, 0U);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t i = 0U; (i < 4U) && sent; i++)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            res[v][i] = IST_AREA->m4_res[i];
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        if (sent)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            LOG("T12 %s: %lu of %lu OK, %lu stalled (ERR_TIMEOUT, then PE = 0), first other status 0x%lX, slowest "
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                "%lu cycles", k_name[v], (unsigned long)res[v][0], (unsigned long)T12_COUNT, (unsigned long)res[v][1],
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                (unsigned long)res[v][2], (unsigned long)res[v][3]);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        (void)delay_ms(500U);                            /* the target's reports drain */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const bool st_ok = sent && host_at("F334 STATUS t12=4");
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    /* Back: I2C1 off the HSI, then the HSI as it was, both read back. */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    (void)inst_deinit(true, &diff);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const bool back = i2c123sel_set((d2_was & RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Msk) >> RCC_D2CCIP2R_I2C123SEL_Pos) &&
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                      (RCC->D2CCIP2R == d2_was) && ((cr_was != 0U) || rcc_cr_set(RCC_CR_HSION, false));
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    verdict(ok && sent && st_ok && (res[0][0] == T12_COUNT) && (res[1][0] == T12_COUNT),
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            "T12 2.19.6 with the kernel on the HSI: driver poll %lu/%lu and DMA %lu/%lu writes of 2 bytes OK (0 stalls)",
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(sent ? res[0][0] : 0U), (unsigned long)T12_COUNT, (unsigned long)(sent ? res[1][0] : 0U),
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)T12_COUNT);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    if (sent)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        const uint32_t man = res[2][1] + res[3][1];
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        LOG("T12 control (decision 6): without the preload, %lu stalls by hand in polling and %lu with DMA in %lu each: "
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            "%s", (unsigned long)res[2][1], (unsigned long)res[3][1], (unsigned long)T12_COUNT,
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            (man != 0U) ? "reproduced" : "no reproducido (not reproduced)");
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    verdict(back, "T12 restore: RCC_D2CCIP2R 0x%08lX (0x%08lX before), RCC_CR HSION %lu (%lu before)",
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)RCC->D2CCIP2R, (unsigned long)d2_was, (unsigned long)((RCC->CR & RCC_CR_HSION) != 0U),
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)(cr_was != 0U));
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +/* ---- The bus steps -------------------------------------------------------------------- */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +/* PB8/PB9 to AF6 (I2C4) through the claim (S15, S16). */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool cm7_pins_af(void)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    (void)I2C_BM_EnableClock(I2C4);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    i2c_release(I2C4);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    return pin_cfg(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SCL, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, IST_AF_I2C4, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +           pin_cfg(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SDA, GPIO_BM_MODE_AF, IST_AF_I2C4, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool cm7_pins_input(void)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    return pin_cfg(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SCL, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE) &&
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +           pin_cfg(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SDA, GPIO_BM_MODE_INPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +/* T9: PB8/PB9 GPIO open-drain, released (ODR = 1 first), claimed together (S16 refuses
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +   a second output pin while the first already is one); step F1_RECOVER only (od_out). */
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool cm7_pins_od(void)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    if (!bench_claim(GPIOB, IST_BUS_MASK, GPIO_BM_MODE_OUTPUT, 0U, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE))
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("claim refused");
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        return false;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    const bool ok = (GPIO_BM_ConfigOutput(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SCL, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 1U) ==
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                     GPIO_BM_OK) &&
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                    (GPIO_BM_ConfigOutput(GPIOB, IST_PIN_SDA, GPIO_BM_OTYPE_OD, GPIO_BM_SPEED_LOW, GPIO_BM_PULL_NONE, 1U) ==
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +                     GPIO_BM_OK);
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +        bench_abort("config");
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    return ok;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static void bus_write(void *ctx, bool high, uint32_t pin)
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    (void)ctx;
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    GPIOB->BSRR = high ? (1UL << pin) : (1UL << (pin + 16U));
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +    __DSB();
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static void rec_scl_w(void *ctx, bool high) { bus_write(ctx, high, IST_PIN_SCL); }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static void rec_sda_w(void *ctx, bool high) { bus_write(ctx, high, IST_PIN_SDA); }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool rec_scl_r(void *ctx) { (void)ctx; return ((GPIOB->IDR >> IST_PIN_SCL) & 1UL) != 0U; }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +static bool rec_sda_r(void *ctx) { (void)ctx; return ((GPIOB->IDR >> IST_PIN_SDA) & 1UL) != 0U; }
[+   1.823s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +static void t9(void)
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t tr = 0U;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t diff = 0U;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t r[IST_XR_N];
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    uint8_t b[2] = { 0x30U, 0x31U };
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!cm7_pins_af() || !main_init(false, 0U, &tr))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("HOLD SDA 300 EDGES 5"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const bool low = wait_pin(IST_PIN_SDA, 0U, 20U);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    (void)delay_ms(1U);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t isr = I2C4->ISR;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t0 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sw = I2C_BM_Write(I2C4, T_ADDR, b, 2U, ms_cyc(5U));
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t cw = DWT->CYCCNT - t0;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    /* The recovery: PE = 0 (Deinit), the pins to input, then GPIO open-drain. */
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    (void)inst_deinit(false, &diff);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    bool ok = cm7_pins_input() && delay_ms(1U) && cm7_pins_od();
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok)
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (s_taps && !host_at("SCOPE T9 k=0 f=0 kernel=0 timingr=0 model=0 rate=2000000 trig=scl"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Recovery_t rec = { rec_scl_w, rec_sda_w, rec_scl_r, rec_sda_r, NULL, 5U * CYC_PER_US };
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t pulses = 0xFFU;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const I2cBm_Status_t sr = I2C_BM_RecoverBus(&rec, ms_cyc(10U), &pulses);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t lv = bus_idr() & 3U;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (s_taps && !host_at("SCOPE end"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    ok = cm7_pins_input() && delay_ms(1U) && cm7_pins_af() && main_init(false, 0U, &tr);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!ok || !host_at("HOLD_END"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!xfer(false, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 2U, 0x00009012UL, budget(s_cpu_hz, s_f_main, 3U), r))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    xline("T9", false, s_f_main, IST_XF(IST_OP_WRITE, IST_MODE_POLL, T_ADDR), 2U, 0x00009012UL, r, true);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    verdict(low && ((isr & I2C_ISR_BUSY) != 0U) && ((sw == I2C_BM_ERR_BUS) || (sw == I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT)) &&
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            (cw <= ms_cyc(6U)) && (sr == I2C_BM_OK) && (pulses == 5U) && (lv == 3U) &&
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            (r[IST_XR_STATUS] == (uint32_t)I2C_BM_OK),
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            "T9 the target holds SDA until 5 SCL edges: I2C4 ISR 0x%08lX (BUSY), Write %u in %lu cycles (ERR_BUS %u or "
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            "ERR_TIMEOUT %u, bounded); PB8/PB9 GPIO open-drain, RecoverBus %u, pulses %lu (5), PB8/PB9 after %lu (3); "
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            "AF6 and Init again, Write %lu (OK)", (unsigned long)isr, (unsigned)sw, (unsigned long)cw,
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned)I2C_BM_ERR_BUS, (unsigned)I2C_BM_ERR_TIMEOUT, (unsigned)sr, (unsigned long)pulses,
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)lv, (unsigned long)r[IST_XR_STATUS]);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    /* The control: no RecoverBus; SDA stays low until the target's own limit. */
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("HOLD SDA 100"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const bool low_c = wait_pin(IST_PIN_SDA, 0U, 20U);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t t1 = DWT->CYCCNT;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const bool high_c = wait_pin(IST_PIN_SDA, 1U, 300U);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    const uint32_t ms = (DWT->CYCCNT - t1) / (s_cpu_hz / 1000U);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!host_at("HOLD_END"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    }
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    verdict(low_c && high_c && (ms >= 95U) && (ms <= 110U),
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            "T9 control without RecoverBus: SDA low %lu ms (the target's 100 ms limit), then released %u",
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +            (unsigned long)ms, (unsigned)high_c);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    (void)inst_deinit(false, &diff);
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    (void)cm7_pins_input();
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +static void step_f1_cm4(void)
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    if (!step_begin("F1_CM4"))
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.824s] [host] diff| +        return;

[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +   same as the target's crc_byte() (tools/i2c_target_f334/src/target.c). */
[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +uint32_t ist_crc32(const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t n)
[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t crc = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t i = 0U; i < n; i++)
[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +    {

[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +        {

[+   1.827s] [host] diff| +        }

[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +/* T1 with BUSY = 1 (the target holds SDA low after a START on the bus): every

[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +   pins in AF. */
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +void ist_t1_busy(I2C_TypeDef *i2c, IstT1Result_t *r)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    uint32_t a[T1_SNAP];

[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->bad = 0U;

[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +/* ---- T2: register images against the RM0399 field table typed here ------------------- */
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +/* CR1 (50:2438-2566): PE bit 0, DNF bits 11:8, ANFOFF bit 12; TIMEOUTR (50:2812-2862):
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +   TIMEOUTA bits 11:0, TIDLE bit 12, TIMOUTEN bit 15; TIMINGR as written (50:2762-2808). */
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +static const uint32_t k_t2_timingr[] = { 0x00000000UL, 0x30C06C81UL, 0x20B0142FUL, 0x00E01931UL, 0xF0FFFFFFUL };
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +static const uint32_t k_t2_dnf[] = { 0U, 1U, 15U };
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +static const uint32_t k_t2_tmo[] = { 0U, 1U, 489U, 4096U };
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +#define T2_N(t)   ((uint32_t)(sizeof(t) / sizeof((t)[0])))
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +static void t2_expect(uint32_t timingr, bool analog, uint32_t dnf, uint32_t tmo, uint32_t e[IST_NREG])
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[0] = 0x00000001UL | (analog ? 0U : (1UL << 12)) | (dnf << 8);
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[1] = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[2] = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[3] = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[4] = timingr;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    e[5] = (tmo == 0U) ? 0U : ((tmo - 1U) | (1UL << 15));
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +}
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +void ist_t2(I2C_TypeDef *i2c, IstT2Result_t *r)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +{
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->cases = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->bad = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->bad_deinit = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->bad_status = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->first_bad = 0xFFFFFFFFUL;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->got = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->exp = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    r->control = 0U;
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    for (uint32_t a = 0U; a < T2_N(k_t2_timingr); a++)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +    {
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +        for (uint32_t f = 0U; f < 2U; f++)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +        {
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +            for (uint32_t d = 0U; d < T2_N(k_t2_dnf); d++)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +            {
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +                for (uint32_t m = 0U; m < T2_N(k_t2_tmo); m++)
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +                {
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +                    const bool analog = (f == 0U);
[+   1.829s] [host] diff| +                    const I2cBm_Config_t c = { i2c, k_t2_timingr[a], analog, k_t2_dnf[d], k_t2_tmo[m] };
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    uint32_t e[IST_NREG];
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    uint32_t g[IST_NREG];
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    t2_expect(k_t2_timingr[a], analog, k_t2_dnf[d], k_t2_tmo[m], e);
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    const I2cBm_Status_t st = I2C_BM_Init(&c);
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    ist_regs(i2c, g);
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    if ((st != I2C_BM_OK) && (st != I2C_BM_ERR_BUS))
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        r->bad_status++;
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    bool same = true;
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    for (uint32_t k = 0U; k < 6U; k++)
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        if (g[k] != e[k])
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                            if (r->first_bad == 0xFFFFFFFFUL)
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                            {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                                r->first_bad = (r->cases << 4) | k;
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                                r->got = g[k];
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                                r->exp = e[k];
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                            }
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                            same = false;
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        }
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    if (!same)
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        r->bad++;
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    }
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    /* The control, once: the same image against an expected one with
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                       DNF + 1 must differ. */
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    if ((r->cases == 0U) && same)
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                    {
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        uint32_t e2[IST_NREG];
[+   1.830s] [host] diff| +                        t2_expect(k_t2_timingr[a], analog, k_t2_dnf[d] + 1U, k_t2_tmo[m], e2);

[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +def calc_timing(k: int, f: int, tr: int, tf: int, af: bool, dnf: int) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    """The plan's calculation rules, written again from the plan (not from
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    i2c_bm.c), with N rounded up as approved on 2026-10-07: (status, TIMINGR,
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    mode, scl_hz_model). Status 1 ERR_PARAM, 3 ERR_RANGE."""
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if k == 0 or f == 0 or f > 1000000 or dnf > 15:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        return ST_PARAM, 0, 0, 0
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    m = 0 if f <= 100000 else (1 if f <= 400000 else 2)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    _, tl, th, tsu, thd, tvd, trm, tfm, kmin = MODES[m][:9]
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if tr > trm or tf > tfm or k < kmin:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        return ST_RANGE, 0, 0, 0
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    afmin, afmax = T_AF if af else (0, 0)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    sync = _cdiv((tr + tf + 2 * afmin) * k, G) + 2 * (dnf + 3)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    for p in range(16):
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        d = G * (p + 1)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        scldel = max(0, _cdiv((tr + tsu) * k, d) - 1)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        if scldel > 15:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +            continue
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        smin = max(0, _cdiv((tf + thd - afmin) * k - (dnf + 3) * G, d))
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        smax = ((tvd - tr - afmax) * k - (dnf + 4) * G) // d
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        if smin > smax or smin > 15:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +            continue
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        n = _cdiv(_cdiv(k, f) - sync, p + 1)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        lmin, hmin = _cdiv(tl * k, d), _cdiv(th * k, d)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        lo = max(lmin, _cdiv(n * tl, tl + th))
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        hi = max(hmin, n - lo)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        if lo > 256 or hi > 256:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +            continue
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        if (lo * (p + 1) - dnf - 4) * G <= afmax * k:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +            continue
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        if hi * (p + 1) <= 1:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +            continue
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        t = (p << 28) | (scldel << 20) | (smin << 16) | ((hi - 1) << 8) | (lo - 1)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        return ST_OK, t, m, k // (sync + (lo + hi) * (p + 1))
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    return ST_RANGE, 0, 0, 0

[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +def timingr_rm_problems(k: int, f: int, tr: int, tf: int, af: bool, dnf: int, t: int, model: int) -> list[str]:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    """What an OK TIMINGR must meet, checked on its fields against RM0399 itself
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    (not against the rules): 50:342 (SCLDEL), 50:319/331 (SDADEL window), the
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    counters at least tLOW/tHIGH of table 407, 50:162-163, the kernel of 2.19.1,
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    reserved bits 0, and the model frequency not above the one asked."""
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    m = 0 if f <= 100000 else (1 if f <= 400000 else 2)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    _, tl, th, tsu, thd, tvd, trm, tfm, kmin = MODES[m][:9]
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    p, scldel, sdadel = (t >> 28) & 0xF, (t >> 20) & 0xF, (t >> 16) & 0xF
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    scll, sclh = (t & 0xFF) + 1, ((t >> 8) & 0xFF) + 1
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    afmin, afmax = T_AF if af else (0, 0)
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    bad = []
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if t & 0x0F000000:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("reserved bits 27:24 set")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if (scldel + 1) * (p + 1) * G < (tr + tsu) * k:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("SCLDEL below t_r + tSU;DAT (50:342)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if sdadel * (p + 1) * G < (tf + thd - afmin) * k - (dnf + 3) * G:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("SDADEL below its minimum (50:319)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if sdadel * (p + 1) * G > (tvd - tr - afmax) * k - (dnf + 4) * G:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("SDADEL above its maximum (50:331)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if scll * (p + 1) * G < tl * k:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("SCLL counter below tLOW (table 407)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if sclh * (p + 1) * G < th * k:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("SCLH counter below tHIGH (table 407)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if 4 * G >= (scll * (p + 1) - dnf) * G - afmax * k:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("t_kernel >= (tLOW - t_filters)/4 (50:162)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if sclh * (p + 1) <= 1:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("t_kernel >= tHIGH (50:163)")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if k < kmin:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append("kernel below ES0445 2.19.1")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    if model > f:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"model {model} above {f}")
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    return bad

[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +def t0b_calc_selftest(log: Callable[[str], None]) -> bool:
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    """T3's two oracles on vectors typed here: the bench's own case (with the
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    plan's floor of N the model came out at 100 100 Hz; rounded up 99 700), the
[+   1.831s] [host] diff| +    RM check of a good TIMINGR, and the two controls of the plan: an expected
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    value altered (SCLL + 1) is told apart, and SDADEL outside its window is
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    refused by the RM check."""
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    ok = True
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    st, t, m, model = calc_timing(100000000, 100000, 250, 20, True, 0)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    good = st == ST_OK and model == 99700 and model <= 100000 and not timingr_rm_problems(
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        100000000, 100000, 250, 20, True, 0, t, model)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok and good
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    log(f"[host] T0B(a) T3 calc: 100 MHz, 100 kHz, t_r 250, t_f 20, filter: status {st} TIMINGR 0x{t:08X} model "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        f"{model} (99700, RM check clean) -> {'as expected' if good else 'WRONG'}")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    altered = t + 1
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    good = altered != t
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok and good
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    log(f"[host] T0B(a) T3 control: expected TIMINGR with SCLL + 1 (0x{altered:08X}) differs from 0x{t:08X}: "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        f"{'told apart' if good else 'NOT told apart'}")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    # With t_f = 300 ns the SDADEL window starts above 0 (50:319): the vector's own
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    # TIMINGR passes, the same with SDADEL = 0 must not.
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    st2, t2, _, model2 = calc_timing(100000000, 100000, 1000, 300, True, 0)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    clean = st2 == ST_OK and not timingr_rm_problems(100000000, 100000, 1000, 300, True, 0, t2, model2)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    bad_sda = t2 & ~(0xF << 16)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    probs = timingr_rm_problems(100000000, 100000, 1000, 300, True, 0, bad_sda, model2)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    good = clean and ((t2 >> 16) & 0xF) > 0 and any("SDADEL" in x for x in probs)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    ok = ok and good
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    log(f"[host] T0B(a) T3 control: 100 MHz, 100 kHz, t_r 1000, t_f 300: TIMINGR 0x{t2:08X} (SDADEL "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        f"{(t2 >> 16) & 0xF}) passes {clean}; with SDADEL 0 (below 50:319) the RM check "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        f"{'refuses: ' + '; '.join(probs) if probs else 'accepts'} -> {'as expected' if good else 'WRONG'}")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    for k, f, want in ((3999999, 100000, ST_RANGE), (100000000, 0, ST_PARAM), (100000000, 1000001, ST_PARAM)):
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        got = calc_timing(k, f, 100, 10, True, 0)[0]
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        good = got == want
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok and good
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        log(f"[host] T0B(a) T3 calc refusal k={k} f={f}: status {got} ({want}) -> {'as expected' if good else 'WRONG'}")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return ok

[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def ist_fill(n: int, seed: int) -> bytes:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    """The selftest's ist_fill(): xorshift32 bytes (bits 31:24 of each state)."""
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    x = seed if seed else 0x2545F491
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    out = bytearray()
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    for _ in range(n):
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        x ^= (x << 13) & 0xFFFFFFFF
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        x ^= x >> 17
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        x ^= (x << 5) & 0xFFFFFFFF
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        out.append((x >> 24) & 0xFF)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return bytes(out)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def target_reg(i: int) -> int:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    """The target's register i after INIT (tools/i2c_target_f334: reg[i] = i ^ 0xA5)."""
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return (i & 0xFF) ^ 0xA5
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def target_bytes(p: int, n: int) -> bytes:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return bytes(target_reg(p + i) for i in range(n))
