[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +X_RX = re.compile(r"\[i2c\] X (\S+) (cm4|cm7) f=(\d+) op=(\S+) mode=(\S+) a=([0-9A-F]+) n=(\d+) p=(\d+) st=(\d+) "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                  r"crc=([0-9A-F]+) mis=(\d+) bad=(-?\d+) cyc=(\d+) dtx=(\d+) drx=(\d+) irq=(\d+) irqa=(\d+) "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                  r"cr1=([0-9A-F]+) sen=(\d+) isr=([0-9A-F]+) aux=(\d+) t=(\d)(?: d=([0-9A-F]+))?")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def parse_x(text: str) -> Optional[dict]:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    m = X_RX.search(text)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    if not m:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        return None
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    g = m.groups()
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return {"test": g[0], "core": g[1], "f": int(g[2]), "op": g[3], "mode": g[4], "a": int(g[5], 16), "n": int(g[6]),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "p": int(g[7]), "st": int(g[8]), "crc": int(g[9], 16), "mis": int(g[10]), "bad": int(g[11]),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "cyc": int(g[12]), "dtx": int(g[13]), "drx": int(g[14]), "irq": int(g[15]), "irqa": int(g[16]),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "cr1": int(g[17], 16), "sen": int(g[18]), "isr": int(g[19], 16), "aux": int(g[20]), "t": int(g[21]),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "d": bytes.fromhex(g[22]) if g[22] else None, "text": text}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +RPT_RX = re.compile(r"^RPT n=(\d+) st=(\d+) sr=(\d+) sp=(\d+) dir=(\S*) rx=(\d+) tx=(\d+) nk=(\d+) nm=(\d+) "
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                    r"crc_rx=([0-9A-F]+) crc_tx=([0-9A-F]+) p0=(\d+) p1=(\d+) ev=([0-9A-F]+) us=(\d+)")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def parse_rpt(text: str) -> Optional[dict]:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    m = RPT_RX.search(text)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    if not m:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        return None
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    g = m.groups()
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return {"seq": int(g[0]), "st": int(g[1]), "sr": int(g[2]), "sp": int(g[3]), "dir": g[4], "rx": int(g[5]),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "tx": int(g[6]), "nk": int(g[7]), "nm": int(g[8]), "crc_rx": int(g[9], 16), "crc_tx": int(g[10], 16),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            "p0": int(g[11]), "p1": int(g[12]), "ev": int(g[13], 16), "us": int(g[14]), "text": text}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    return zlib.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +def x_expect(x: dict) -> dict:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    """What the host itself expects of a transfer the selftest made (independent of
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    the firmware's own verdicts): its status, the data, and the target's report.
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    x["armed"] is what the host armed in the target for this transfer (NACK k,
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    CORRUPT k), from its own AT handling."""
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    test, op, n, p = x["test"], x["op"], x["n"], x["p"]
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    armed = x.get("armed") or {}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    e: dict = {"st": ST_OK, "rpt": None}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    if op == "W":
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        data = ist_fill(n, p)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        e["data"] = data
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        e["crc"] = crc32(data)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = {"st": 1, "sr": 0, "sp": 1, "dir": "W", "rx": n, "tx": 0, "nk": 0, "crc_rx": crc32(data), "ev": 0}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        k = armed.get("nack")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        if k is not None and k < n:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            # The target NACKs data byte k: bytes 0..k received, byte k refused (R2-2)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["st"] = ST_NACK
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["rpt"] = {"st": 1, "sr": 0, "sp": 1, "dir": "W", "rx": k + 1, "tx": 0, "nk": 1,
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                        "crc_rx": crc32(data[:k + 1]), "ev": 0}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            if x["mode"] == "it":
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                e["dtx"] = k
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +    elif op in ("R", "WR"):
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        data = bytearray(target_bytes(p, n))
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        k = armed.get("corrupt")
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        if k is not None and k < n:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            data[k] ^= 0xFF                              # the target inverts the k-th byte it sends
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["mis"] = 1
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["bad"] = k
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        data = bytes(data)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        e["data"] = data
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        e["crc"] = crc32(data)
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        if op == "R":
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["rpt"] = {"st": 1, "sr": 0, "sp": 1, "dir": "R", "rx": 0, "tx": n, "nm": 1, "crc_tx": crc32(data),
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +                        "ev": 0, "p0": p & 0xFF}
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +        else:
[+   1.832s] [host] diff| +            e["rpt"] = {"st": 2, "sr": 1, "sp": 1, "dir": "WR", "rx": 1, "tx": n, "nm": 1,
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                        "crc_rx": crc32(bytes([p & 0xFF])), "crc_tx": crc32(data), "ev": 0, "p1": (p + n) & 0xFF}
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    elif op == "P":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = {"st": 1, "sr": 0, "sp": 1, "dir": "W", "rx": 0, "tx": 0, "ev": 0x10}
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["a"] == NO_TARGET:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["st"] = ST_NACK
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = None
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if test == "T6C":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["st"] = ST_TIMEOUT
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["aux"] = ST_PENDING
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = {"sp": 0, "ev_has": 0x08, "dir": "W", "rx_max": 2}
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if test == "T7C":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["st"] = ST_TIMEOUT
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["sen"] = n
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = {"sp": 0, "ev_has": 0x08, "dir": "R", "tx_max": 3}
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if test.startswith("T10B"):
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["st"] = ST_BUS if test == "T10B" else ST_TIMEOUT
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        e["rpt"] = None                                  # the target is off while it holds SCL
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return e
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +def x_problems(x: dict, rpt: Optional[dict]) -> list[str]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    """Everything that differs between a transfer as the selftest reports it, as
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    the host expects it, and as the target saw it."""
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    e = x_expect(x)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    bad = []
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["st"] != e["st"]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"status {ST.get(x['st'], x['st'])} ({ST[e['st']]})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if "crc" in e and e["st"] == ST_OK and x["crc"] != e["crc"]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"crc {x['crc']:08X} (host {e['crc']:08X})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["op"] in ("R", "WR") and e["st"] == ST_OK and (x["mis"], max(x["bad"], -1)) != (e.get("mis", 0), e.get("bad", -1)):
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"{x['mis']} bytes differ from the target's known content, first at {x['bad']} "
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                   f"(the host expects {e.get('mis', 0)} at {e.get('bad', -1)})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["d"] is not None and "data" in e and x["d"] != e["data"][:len(x["d"])]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"d={x['d'].hex().upper()} (host {e['data'][:len(x['d'])].hex().upper()})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if "dtx" in e and x["dtx"] != e["dtx"]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"acknowledged count {x['dtx']} ({e['dtx']})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if "aux" in e and x["aux"] != e["aux"]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"aux {x['aux']} ({e['aux']})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if "sen" in e and x["sen"] != e["sen"]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"sentinel bytes {x['sen']} ({e['sen']})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["mode"] == "it" and (x["irqa"] != 0 or (x["cr1"] & 0xFE) != 0):
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"after the end: IRQs {x['irqa']}, CR1 0x{x['cr1']:08X}")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if x["mode"] == "dma" and x["aux"] != 0:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"DMA steps 0x{x['aux']:X}")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    want = e["rpt"]
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if (x["t"] == 1) != (want is not None) and not x["test"].startswith("T10B"):
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        bad.append(f"t={x['t']} but the host expects {'a' if want else 'no'} report")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    if want is not None:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        if rpt is None:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            bad.append("no RPT from the target")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        else:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            for key, val in want.items():
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                if key == "ev_has":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                    if not rpt["ev"] & val:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                        bad.append(f"RPT ev 0x{rpt['ev']:02X} without 0x{val:02X}")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                elif key == "rx_max":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                    if rpt["rx"] > val:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                        bad.append(f"RPT rx {rpt['rx']} (<= {val})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                elif key == "tx_max":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                    if rpt["tx"] > val:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                        bad.append(f"RPT tx {rpt['tx']} (<= {val})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                elif rpt.get(key) != val:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                    got = rpt.get(key)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                    bad.append(f"RPT {key}={got:08X} ({val:08X})" if key.startswith("crc") else f"RPT {key}={got} ({val})")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return bad
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +def t0b_pairing_selftest(log: Callable[[str], None]) -> bool:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    """The X/RPT rules on lines typed here: a good read, its target report with the
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    CRC of the bytes it sent, and four faults that must be found."""
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    data = target_bytes(0, 4)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    x = (f"[i2c] X T4 cm7 f=400000 op=R mode=poll a=42 n=4 p=0 st=0 crc={crc32(data):08X} mis=0 bad=-1 cyc=1 "
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +         f"dtx=0 drx=4 irq=0 irqa=0 cr1=00000001 sen=0 isr=00000001 aux=0 t=1")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    r = (f"RPT n=1 st=1 sr=0 sp=1 dir=R rx=0 tx=4 nk=0 nm=1 crc_rx=FFFFFFFF crc_tx={crc32(data):08X} p0=0 p1=4 "
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +         f"ev=00 us=100")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    cases = [(x, r, True, "a good read"),
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +             (x, r.replace("tx=4", "tx=5"), False, "the target sent 5"),
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +             (x, r.replace(f"crc_tx={crc32(data):08X}", "crc_tx=00000000"), False, "another CRC at the target"),
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +             (x.replace("st=0", "st=7"), r, False, "status NACK"),
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +             (x, None, False, "no report")]
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    ok = True
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    for xt, rt, want, why in cases:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        px = parse_x(xt)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        pr = parse_rpt(rt) if rt else None
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        probs = x_problems(px, pr) if px else ["X line not parsed"]
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        good = (not probs) == want
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok and good
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        log(f"[host] T0B(a) X/RPT pairing [{why}]: {'accepted' if not probs else 'refused: ' + '; '.join(probs)} -> "
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            f"{'as expected' if good else 'WRONG'}")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return ok
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +def expected_frames(xs: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list[tuple[int, int]], bool]]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    """The bus frames a group of transfers must show: one entry per START/Sr, its
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    bytes with the acknowledge bit (0 ACK, 1 NACK), and whether a STOP ends it."""
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    out = []
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    for x in xs:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        a, op, n, p = x["a"], x["op"], x["n"], x["p"]
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        nack_addr = 1 if a == NO_TARGET else 0
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        if op == "P":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            out.append(("S", [(a << 1, nack_addr)], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        elif op == "W":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            d = ist_fill(n, p)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            k = (x.get("armed") or {}).get("nack")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            if k is not None and k < n:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                out.append(("S", [(a << 1, 0)] + [(b, 0) for b in d[:k]] + [(d[k], 1)], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            elif nack_addr:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                out.append(("S", [(a << 1, 1)], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            else:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                out.append(("S", [(a << 1, 0)] + [(b, 0) for b in d], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        elif op == "R":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            d = target_bytes(p, n)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            out.append(("S", [((a << 1) | 1, 0)] + [(b, 1 if i == n - 1 else 0) for i, b in enumerate(d)], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +        elif op == "WR":
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            d = target_bytes(p, n)
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            out.append(("S", [(a << 1, 0), (p & 0xFF, 0)], False))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +            out.append(("Sr", [((a << 1) | 1, 0)] + [(b, 1 if i == n - 1 else 0) for i, b in enumerate(d)], True))
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return out
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +def frames_of(dec: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[tuple[int, int]], bool]]:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return [(f["kind"], list(f["bytes"]), f["stop"]) for f in dec["frames"]]
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +def frames_text(fr: list[tuple[str, list[tuple[int, int]], bool]]) -> str:
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +    return " | ".join(f"{k} " + " ".join(f"{v:02X}{'N' if a else 'A'}" for v, a in b) + (" P" if s else "")
[+   1.833s] [host] diff| +                      for k, b, s in fr) or "(nothing)"
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +def t0b_decoder_selftest(log: Callable[[str], None]) -> bool:
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    """ad2.i2c_decode_analog() on a synthetic write-read before the instrument is
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    opened: decoded exactly; with SCL and SDA swapped (T5's control) it is not."""
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    want = [("S", [(0x84, 0), (0x07, 0)], False), ("Sr", [(0x85, 0), (0xA2, 1)], True)]
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    syms = [("S", [(0x84 << 1) | 0, (0x07 << 1) | 0], False), ("Sr", [(0x85 << 1) | 0, (0xA2 << 1) | 1], True)]
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    ok = True
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    for rate, bit, rise in ((20e6, 10e-6, 250e-9), (50e6, 2.5e-6, 250e-9)):
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        scl, sda = ad2mod.i2c_synth_analog(syms, rate, bit, VDD, rise)
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        got = frames_of(ad2mod.i2c_decode_analog(scl, sda, rate, VDD))
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        swapped = frames_of(ad2mod.i2c_decode_analog(sda, scl, rate, VDD))
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        good = got == want and swapped != want
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        ok = ok and good
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        log(f"[host] T0B(a) decoder on a synthetic capture ({rate / 1e6:.0f} MS/s, {1 / bit / 1e3:.0f} kHz): "
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +            f"'{frames_text(got)}'; swapped '{frames_text(swapped)[:40]}' -> {'as expected' if good else 'WRONG'}")
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    return ok
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +def t5_timing_problems(dec: dict, f: int, k: int, t: int, model: int, ratio: float, rate: float) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    """T5's times of one capture: every measured time at least the mode's minimum
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    (tables 405/407), tLOW and tHIGH between their counters (SCLL, SCLH) and the
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    counters plus the synchronisation of CalcTiming's model (recomputed with the
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    t_r and t_f measured here), f_SCL not above the one asked and within 5 % of the
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    model, both normalised by the CPU clock ratio (CLKREF, plan N1). Tolerance per
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    interval: 2 x (2 samples + 2 kernel cycles)."""
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    m = 0 if f <= 100000 else (1 if f <= 400000 else 2)
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    _, tl_min, th_min, tsu_min, _, _, tr_max, tf_max, _, thdsta_min, tsusta_min, tsusto_min, tbuf_min = MODES[m]
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    ns = 1e-9
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    bad: list[str] = []
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +    def med(v: list) -> float:
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        v = sorted(v)
[+   1.834s] [host] diff| +        return v[len(v) // 2] if v else float("nan")

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        rises = [e["t"] for e in se if e["rise"]]
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        sda_rise = [e["t"] for e in de if e["rise"]]
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        sda_fall = [e["t"] for e in de if not e["rise"]]
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        # SDA released after the 5th SCL rise and before a 6th; then the STOP (SDA low
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        # and high again while SCL stays high).
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        rel = sda_rise[0] if sda_rise else None
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        before = sum(1 for t in rises if rel is not None and t < rel)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        stop_ok = len(sda_fall) >= 1 and len(sda_rise) >= 2 and rel is not None and sda_fall[-1] > rel
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        ok = d0 == 0 and before == 5 and len(falls) == 5 and stop_ok
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        self.log(f"[host] {name}: SDA low at the start {d0 == 0}, SCL pulses {len(falls)} falls / {len(rises)} rises, "
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +                 f"SDA released after {before} rises, then SDA falls {len(sda_fall)} rises {len(sda_rise)} (the STOP)")
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        self.group("F1 T9 scope: recovery", ok, f"{name}: {len(falls)} SCL pulses, SDA released after {before}, "
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +                   f"STOP seen {stop_ok}")

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    # -- the AT points ----------------------------------------------------------------
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +    def at_action(self, tag: str) -> tuple[Optional[Callable[[], None]], Optional[Callable[[], None]]]:
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        """(before the handshake, after it) for one AT tag."""
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        m = re.match(r"F334 (.+)$", tag)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if m:
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            cmd = m.group(1).split()

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        m = re.match(r"HOLD (SDA|SCL) (\d+)(?: EDGES (\d+))?$", tag)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if m:

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if tag == "HOLD_END":

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if m:

[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +                    "timingr": int(m.group(5), 16), "model": int(m.group(6)), "rate": int(m.group(7)),
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +                    "trig": m.group(8)}
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            if self.state != "taps":
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +                raise BenchAbort(f"S19: a scope capture announced without taps: {tag}")
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            return (lambda: self.scope_arm(spec)), None
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if tag == "SCOPE end":
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +            return None, None
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        m = re.match(r"CLKREF cyc=(\d+) cpu=(\d+)$", tag)
[+   1.839s] [host] diff| +        if m:

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            box: dict = {}

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        if tag == "RPT lenient":

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        if tag == "RPT strict":
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            return None, (lambda: setattr(self, "lenient", False))

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +    def f334_cmd(self, cmd: list[str]) -> None:
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        c = cmd[0].upper()
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        if c == "INIT" and len(cmd) == 1:
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.f334.command("INIT", r"^OK INIT")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.armed = {}
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        elif c in ("NACK", "CORRUPT") and len(cmd) == 2:
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.f334.command(f"{c} {int(cmd[1])}", rf"^OK {c} ")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.armed[c.lower()] = int(cmd[1])
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        elif c == "STRETCH" and len(cmd) == 3 and cmd[1] in ("ADDR", "BYTE"):
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.f334.command(f"STRETCH {cmd[1]} {int(cmd[2])}", r"^OK STRETCH ")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.armed["stretch"] = True
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        elif c == "FMP" and len(cmd) == 2 and cmd[1] in ("0", "1"):
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.f334.command(f"FMP {cmd[1]}", rf"^OK FMP {cmd[1]}")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        elif c == "STATUS" and len(cmd) == 2 and cmd[1].startswith("t12="):
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            m = self.f334.command("STATUS", r"^STATUS xfers=(\d+) rx=(\d+) tx=(\d+) nk=(\d+) ev=(\d+) idle_cuts=(\d+) "
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +                                            r"dropped=(\d+) uart_ovr=(\d+)")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            self.t12_status[int(cmd[1][4:])] = [int(v) for v in m.groups()]
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        else:
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +            raise BenchAbort(f"F334 command not on the host's list: {' '.join(cmd)}")
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +    def hold_start(self, line: str, ms: int, edges: int) -> None:
[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +        mark = len(self.f334.lines)

[+   1.840s] [host] diff| +    def hold_end(self) -> None:

[+   1.841s] [host] diff| +    def clkref_arm(self, box: dict) -> None:
